263	    if (this.flyTime <= 0) return false;
264	    this.flyTime--;
265	    return true;
266	  }
267	
268	  resetFlightTime(p: MountedPlayerView): void {
269	    const d = this.data;
270	    this.flyTime = this.active ? d?.flightTimeMax ?? 0 : 0;
271	    // 驯鹿：落地瞬间按水平速度续飞（:5267 flyTime += |vx|*20）
272	    if (this.type === 0) this.flyTime += Math.trunc(Math.abs(p.vx) * 20);
273	  }
274	
275	  // ---- 悬停物理（Mount.cs Hover :3310-3466 主体 1:1） ----
276	  hover(p: MountedPlayerView): boolean {
277	    const d = this.data;
278	    if (!d) return false;
279	    const noFatigue = this.hoverIgnoresFatigue();
280	    // 悬停生效帧态（:3313-3332）：默认空中/游泳；熔岩鲨限游泳；56/61 蝙蝠/精灵族含飞行
281	    let flyingState = this.frameState === FRAME_IN_AIR || this.frameState === FRAME_SWIMMING;
282	    if (this.type === 49) flyingState = this.frameState === FRAME_SWIMMING;
283	    if (this.type === 56 || this.type === 61) {
284	      flyingState = this.frameState === FRAME_IN_AIR || this.frameState === FRAME_FLYING;
285	    }
286	    if (flyingState) {
287	      let canLift = true;
288	      let mult = 1;
289	      // 猪鲨崽未湿身减半（:3344-3348 MountFishronSpecial 未实装走减半支线）
290	      if (this.type === 12 && p.inWater === false) mult = 0.5;
291	      const fatigueRatio = noFatigue ? 0 : this.fatigueMax > 0 ? this.fatigue / this.fatigueMax : 0;
292	      // 能量池：先吃 flyTime，尽后累积疲劳，疲劳满则失去升力（:3322-3342）
293	      if (!noFatigue) {
294	        if (this.flyTime > 0) this.flyTime--;
295	        else if (this.fatigue < this.fatigueMax) this.fatigue += 1;
296	        else canLift = false;
297	      }
298	      let vMin = 4 * fatigueRatio;
299	      let vMax = 4 * fatigueRatio;
300	      if (vMin === 0) vMin = -0.001;
301	      if (vMax === 0) vMax = -0.001;
302	      let vy = p.vy;
303	      // 升降输入（:3396-3424）
304	      if ((p.inputUp || p.inputJump) && canLift) {
305	        vMin = -2 - 6 * (1 - fatigueRatio);
306	        if (this.type === 56 || this.type === 61) vMin = -d.dashSpeed!;
307	        vy -= d.acceleration * mult;
308	      } else if (p.inputDown) {
309	        vMax = 8;
310	        if (this.type === 56 || this.type === 61) vMax = d.dashSpeed!;
311	        vy += d.acceleration * mult;
312	      }
313	      // 钳位带（:3426-3436：超带按 acceleration 回拉）
314	      if (vy < vMin) {
315	        vy = (vMin - vy < d.acceleration) ? vMin : vy + d.acceleration * mult;
316	      } else if (vy > vMax) {
317	        vy = (vy - vMax < d.acceleration) ? vMax : vy - d.acceleration * mult;
318	      }
319	      if (this.type === 56 || this.type === 61) {
320	        if (vMin !== -0.001) vy = Math.max(vy, vMin);
321	        if (vMax !== -0.001) vy = Math.min(vy, vMax);
322	      }
323	      p.vy = vy;
324	    } else if (!noFatigue) {
325	      // 非悬停帧态：正常重力（:3440）
326	      p.vy += DEFAULT_GRAVITY * p.gravDir;
327	    }
328	    return true;
329	  }
330	
331	  /** 起跳瞬间向上的 hover 触发（Player.cs:26416：vy==0 且 CanHover 且按上且松键边沿） */
332	  tryStartHover(p: MountedPlayerView): void {
333	    if (p.vy === 0 && this.canHover(p) && p.inputUp && !p.prevInputJump) {
334	      p.vy = -(this.acceleration + DEFAULT_GRAVITY + 0.001);
335	    }
336	  }
337	
338	  /** 退出飞行帧态探测（Mount.cs TryBeginningFlight :4509-4528） */
339	  tryBeginningFlight(p: MountedPlayerView, state: number): void {
340	    if (this.frameState === state || (state !== FRAME_IN_AIR && state !== FRAME_FLYING)
341	      || !this.canHover(p) || p.inputUp || p.inputDown || p.inputJump) return;
342	    const st = p.store;
343	    if (!st) return;
344	    const ty = Math.floor((p.y + p.h + 1) / 16);
345	    let blocked = false;
346	    for (let tx = Math.floor(p.x / 16); tx <= Math.floor((p.x + p.w - 1) / 16); tx++) {
347	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { blocked = true; break; }
348	    }
349	    if (blocked && this.hoverIgnoresFatigue()) p.y -= 0.001;
350	  }
351	
352	  /** 落地帧态探测（Mount.cs TryLanding :4544-4558：无升降输入且下方 4px 无地面） */
353	  tryLanding(p: MountedPlayerView): void {
354	    if ((this.frameState === FRAME_FLYING || this.frameState === FRAME_IN_AIR)
355	      && !p.inputUp && !p.inputDown && !p.inputJump) {
356	      const st = p.store;
357	      if (!st) return;
358	      const ty = Math.floor((p.y + p.h + 4) / 16);
359	      let grounded = false;
360	      for (let tx = Math.floor(p.x / 16); tx <= Math.floor((p.x + p.w - 1) / 16); tx++) {
361	        if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { grounded = true; break; }
362	      }
363	      if (!grounded) this.updateFrame(p, this.intendedGroundedFrame(p), p.vx, p.vy);
364	    }
365	  }
366	
367	  /** 地面帧态意图（Mount.cs GetIntendedGroundedFrame :4529-4543） */
368	  intendedGroundedFrame(p: MountedPlayerView): number {
369	    const still = p.vx === 0;
370	    if (still) return FRAME_STANDING;
371	    return FRAME_RUNNING;
372	  }
373	
374	  // ---- 帧状态机（Mount.cs UpdateFrame :3563-4490 的通用路径 + 常用族特例） ----
375	  updateFrame(p: MountedPlayerView, state: number, vx: number, vy: number): void {
376	    const d = this.data;
377	    if (!d) return;
378	    if (this.frameState !== state) {
379	      this.frameState = state;
380	      this.frameCounter = 0;
381	    }
382	    // UFO（7）帧组恒取空中组（:3688 case 7: state = 2）——frameState 本身不变
383	    if (this.type === 7) state = FRAME_IN_AIR;
384	    if (state !== FRAME_STANDING) this.idleTime = 0;
385	    // 离地行走宽限（:3599-3607）
386	    if (vy === 0) this.walkingGraceTime = d.walkingGraceTimeMax ?? 0;
387	    else if (this.walkingGraceTime > 0) this.walkingGraceTime--;
388	    if (p.inputDown && p.vy > 0) this.walkingGraceTime = 0;
389	
390	    switch (state) {
391	      case FRAME_STANDING: {
392	        // 待机帧（:4139-4226）：站立 delay 计数 + idle 段（rand(900,1500) 后播 idle 动画）
393	        if ((d.idleFrameCount ?? 0) !== 0) {
394	          if (this.idleTime === 0) {
395	            this.idleTimeNext = 900 + Math.trunc(this.idleRand * 600);
396	            if (this.type === 2) this.idleTimeNext = 600 + Math.trunc(this.idleRand * 300);
397	            this.idleRand = Math.random();
398	          }
399	          this.idleTime++;
400	        }
401	        this.frameCounter += 1;
402	        if ((d.idleFrameCount ?? 0) !== 0 && this.idleTime >= this.idleTimeNext) {
403	          const delay = d.idleFrameDelay ?? 12;
404	          const step = Math.trunc((this.idleTime - this.idleTimeNext) / delay);
405	          if (step >= (d.idleFrameCount ?? 0)) {
406	            if (d.idleFrameLoop) { this.idleTime = this.idleTimeNext; this.frame = d.idleFrameStart ?? 0; }
407	            else { this.frameCounter = 0; this.frame = d.standingFrameStart ?? 0; this.idleTime = 0; }
408	          } else {
409	            this.frame = (d.idleFrameStart ?? 0) + step;
410	          }
411	        } else if (this.frameCounter > (d.standingFrameDelay ?? 12)) {
412	          this.frameCounter -= d.standingFrameDelay ?? 12;
413	          this.frame++;
414	        }
415	        if (this.frame < (d.standingFrameStart ?? 0)
416	          || this.frame >= (d.standingFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.standingFrameCount ?? 1)) {
417	          this.frame = d.standingFrameStart ?? 0;
418	        }
419	        break;
420	      }
421	      case FRAME_RUNNING: {
422	        // 跑动帧速率 = 水平速度（:4228-4262）；海盗船/暗黑魔法书族取速度比例
423	        let rate = Math.abs(vx);
424	        if (this.type === 44) rate = Math.max(1, Math.abs(vx) * 0.25);
425	        if (this.type === 48) rate = Math.max(0.5, Math.hypot(vx, vy) * 0.125);
426	        if (this.type === 50) rate = Math.abs(vx) * 0.5;
427	        this.frameCounter += rate;
428	        if (this.frameCounter > (d.runningFrameDelay ?? 12)) {
429	          this.frameCounter -= d.runningFrameDelay ?? 12;
430	          this.frame++;
431	        }
432	        if (this.frame < (d.runningFrameStart ?? 0)
433	          || this.frame >= (d.runningFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.runningFrameCount ?? 1)) {
434	          this.frame = d.runningFrameStart ?? 0;
435	        }
436	        break;
437	      }
438	      case FRAME_FLYING: {
439	        this.frameCounter += 1;
440	        if (this.frameCounter > (d.flyingFrameDelay ?? 12)) {
441	          this.frameCounter -= d.flyingFrameDelay ?? 12;
442	          this.frame++;
443	        }
444	        if (this.frame < (d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0)
445	          || this.frame >= (d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.flyingFrameCount ?? 1)) {
446	          this.frame = d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0;
447	        }
448	        break;
449	      }
450	      case FRAME_IN_AIR: {
451	        this.frameCounter += 1;
452	        if (this.frameCounter > (d.inAirFrameDelay ?? 12)) {
453	          this.frameCounter -= d.inAirFrameDelay ?? 12;
454	          this.frame++;
455	        }
456	        if (this.frame < (d.inAirFrameStart ?? 0)
457	          || this.frame >= (d.inAirFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.inAirFrameCount ?? 1)) {
458	          this.frame = d.inAirFrameStart ?? 0;
459	        }
460	        // 蜜蜂悬停副帧（翅膀层 :4412-4425：疲劳越低拍越快）
461	        if (this.type === 5 && (d.fatigueMax ?? 0) > 0) {
462	          const t = this.fatigue / this.fatigueMax;
463	          this.frameExtraCounter += 6 - 4 * t;
464	          if (this.frameExtraCounter > (d.flyingFrameDelay ?? 12)) {
465	            this.frameExtra++;
466	            this.frameExtraCounter -= d.flyingFrameDelay ?? 12;
467	          }
468	          if (this.frameExtra < (d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0)
469	            || this.frameExtra >= (d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.flyingFrameCount ?? 1)) {
470	            this.frameExtra = d.flyingFrameStart ?? 0;
471	          }
472	        }
473	        break;
474	      }
475	      case FRAME_SWIMMING: {
476	        // 游泳帧速率 = 平均速度（:4427-4446）
477	        const rate = Math.trunc((Math.abs(vx) + Math.abs(vy)) / 2);
478	        this.frameCounter += rate;
479	        if (this.frameCounter > (d.swimFrameDelay ?? 12)) {
480	          this.frameCounter -= d.swimFrameDelay ?? 12;
481	          this.frame++;
482	        }
483	        if (this.frame < (d.swimFrameStart ?? 0)
484	          || this.frame >= (d.swimFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.swimFrameCount ?? 1)) {
485	          this.frame = d.swimFrameStart ?? 0;
486	        }
487	        break;
488	      }
489	      case FRAME_DASHING: {
490	        this.frameCounter += Math.abs(vx);
491	        if (this.frameCounter > (d.dashingFrameDelay ?? 40)) {
492	          this.frameCounter -= d.dashingFrameDelay ?? 40;
493	          this.frame++;
494	        }
495	        if (this.frame < (d.dashingFrameStart ?? 0)
496	          || this.frame >= (d.dashingFrameStart ?? 0) + Math.max(1, d.dashingFrameCount ?? 1)) {
497	          this.frame = d.dashingFrameStart ?? 0;
498	        }
499	        break;
500	      }
501	    }
502	  }
503	
504	  /** 每帧帧态调度（Player.cs:35745-35790 的 mount.UpdateFrame 分派 1:1）。
505	   *  飞行帧态门 = FlyTime>0 && jump==0 && controlJump && !CanHover（:35734） */
506	  updateFrameDispatch(p: MountedPlayerView): void {
507	    if (p.vy !== 0 && this.walkingGraceTime <= 0) {
508	      if (this.flyTime > 0 && p.inputJump && !this.canHover(p)) {
509	        this.tryBeginningFlight(p, FRAME_FLYING);
510	        this.updateFrame(p, FRAME_FLYING, p.vx, p.vy);
511	        this.tryLanding(p);
512	      } else if (p.inWater) {
513	        this.updateFrame(p, FRAME_SWIMMING, p.vx, p.vy);
514	      } else {
515	        this.tryBeginningFlight(p, FRAME_IN_AIR);
516	        this.updateFrame(p, FRAME_IN_AIR, p.vx, p.vy);
517	        this.tryLanding(p);
518	      }
519	    } else {
520	      this.updateFrame(p, this.intendedGroundedFrame(p), p.vx, p.vy);
521	    }
522	  }
523	
524	  // ---- 上下坐骑 ----
525	
526	  /** 上坐骑（Mount.cs SetMount :6184-6296）：同型/无效 id/蜜蜂浸水 拒绝；
527	   *  换骑先复位旧实例。坐骑 buff（AddBuff(buff,3600)）不入 BuffState——原版该 buff
528	   *  是 TimeLeftDoesNotDecrease 的占位（每 tick 刷回 10t，掉 buff 即下坐），本仓以
529	   *  mount.active 单一真源表达，buff 附带效果（131/168/265 的 ignoreWater 等）由
530	   *  swimFree/免淹门直接消费；出生尘（DoSpawnDust :6363）经 game 回调 */
531	  setMount(m: number, p: MountedPlayerView & { buffs?: { remove(t: unknown): void } }, game?: {
532	    spawnParticles?(x: number, y: number, color: string, n: number, spd?: number): void;
533	  }): boolean {
534	    if (this.type === m || m < 0 || m >= MOUNT_COUNT) return false;
535	    if (m === 5 && p.inWater) return false;              // 蜜蜂座骑浸水不可上（:6190）
536	    if (this.active) {
537	      this.reset();
538	    }
539	    this.active = true;
540	    this.flyTime = 0;
541	    this.type = m;
542	    const d = this.data;
543	    this.fatigueMax = d?.fatigueMax ?? 0;
544	    // 出生尘（DoSpawnDust :6363：spawnDust 族；本仓粒子为程序化圆，数量 10）
545	    if (game?.spawnParticles && d?.spawnDust) {
546	      game.spawnParticles(p.x + p.w / 2, p.y + p.h / 2, '#C8C8C8', 10, 1.5);
547	    }
548	    return true;
549	  }
550	
551	  /** 下坐空间检查（Mount.cs CanDismountWithResult :6600：42 高玩家盒能放下才许下） */
552	  canDismount(p: MountedPlayerView): boolean {
553	    const st = p.store;
554	    if (!st) return true;
555	    const h = 42;
556	    for (let tx = Math.floor(p.x / 16); tx <= Math.floor((p.x + p.w - 1) / 16); tx++) {
557	      for (let ty = Math.floor((p.y + p.h - h) / 16); ty <= Math.floor((p.y + p.h - 1) / 16); ty++) {
558	        if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return false;
559	      }
560	    }
561	    return true;
562	  }
563	
564	  tryDismount(p: MountedPlayerView): boolean {
565	    if (!this.canDismount(p)) return false;
566	    this.dismount(p);
567	    return true;
568	  }
569	
570	  dismount(p?: MountedPlayerView, game?: {
571	    spawnParticles?(x: number, y: number, color: string, n: number, spd?: number): void;
572	  }): void {
573	    if (!this.active) return;
574	    if (game?.spawnParticles && this.data?.spawnDust) {
575	      game.spawnParticles((p?.x ?? 0) + (p?.w ?? 0) / 2, (p?.y ?? 0) + (p?.h ?? 0) / 2, '#C8C8C8', 10, 1.5);
576	    }
577	    this.reset();
578	  }
579	
580	  reset(): void {
581	    this.active = false;
582	    this.type = -1;
583	    this.frame = 0;
584	    this.frameCounter = 0;
585	    this.frameExtra = 0;
586	    this.frameExtraCounter = 0;
587	    this.frameState = FRAME_STANDING;
588	    this.flyTime = 0;
589	    this.fatigue = 0;
590	    this.fatigueMax = 0;
591	    this.idleTime = 0;
592	    this.idleTimeNext = -1;
593	    this.abilityCharging = false;
594	    this.abilityCharge = 0;
595	    this.abilityCooldown = 0;
596	    this.abilityDuration = 0;
597	    this.walkingGraceTime = 0;
598	  }
599	
600	  /** 每 tick 维护（Player.fixedUpdate 挂点）：能量恢复 + 地面飞行时量回充 + 帧态机 */
601	  upkeep(p: MountedPlayerView): void {
602	    if (!this.active || this.isCart) return;
603	    this.abilityRecovery();
604	    if (p.onGround || p.vy === 0) {
605	      this.resetFlightTime(p);
606	      this.fatigueRecovery();
607	    }
608	    this.tryStartHover(p);
609	    this.updateFrameDispatch(p);
610	  }
611	
612	  // ---- 渲染信息 ----
613	
614	  /** 玩家绘制纵向偏移（Mount.PlayerOffset :409-423：playerYOffsets[当前帧]） */
615	  get playerOffset(): number {
616	    const d = this.data;
617	    if (!d || !d.playerYOffsets?.length) return 0;
618	    if (this.frame >= d.totalFrames) return 0;
619	    return d.playerYOffsets[this.frame] ?? 0;
620	  }
621	
622	  get textures(): { back?: string; front?: string; backExtra?: string; frontExtra?: string } {
623	    const d = this.data;
624	    if (!d) return {};
625	    const f = (n?: string) => (n ? `vanilla/${n}` : undefined);
626	    return { back: f(d.backTexture), front: f(d.frontTexture), backExtra: f(d.backTextureExtra), frontExtra: f(d.frontTextureExtra) };
627	  }
628	}
629	
630	/** 物品 id（vanilla）→ 坐骑 id；非召唤物返回 -1 */
631	export function mountOfItem(vanillaItemId: number): number {
632	  const m = ITEM_TO_MOUNT[String(vanillaItemId)];
633	  return m === undefined ? -1 : m;
634	}
635	
636	/** 坐骑 buff id → 坐骑 id（BuffID.Sets.MountType） */
637	export function mountOfBuff(buffId: number): number {
638	  const m = BUFF_TO_MOUNT[String(buffId)];
639	  return m === undefined ? -1 : m;
640	}
641	

```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T08:37:57.520Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
KeyR handling:
1. Riding minecart → dismount ✓
2. Riding mount → tryDismountMount ✓
3. Not riding + track near → QuickMinecart

But vanilla QuickMount (the R key / "Quick Mount" hotkey) semantics:
- If mounted: TryDismount (with FailedNoSpace counter, 3 fails → teleport).
- If not mounted: gates then GetItemToUse: miscEquips[3] (mount equip slot) first if not cart, else inventory scan for non-cart mount item → SetMount. ONLY if no item found → QuickMinecart().

The Game.ts implementation: R never summons a mount from inventory! It only: minecart dismount, mount dismount, quick minecart. There's no QuickMount_GetItemToUse path on KeyR. Let me double-check by searching for where KeyR triggers summon — search "QuickMount" or mount summon on key. Let me grep for ridingMount and setMount callers.

Also note: useMountItem is invoked on left-click use of mount items (line 4906-4909). So summoning happens by using the item (matching Player.cs:42095 path ✓), and R is dismount + quick minecart. In vanilla, R is "Quick Mount" which summons if you have a mount item. The implementation's R = dismount/quickminecart only. Deviation: missing the "R 召唤坐骑（QuickMount_GetItemToUse 装备槽→背包）" branch.

Hmm wait — the task says "Game.ts 的 useMountItem/KeyR 召唤段". Let me check if there's another KeyR handler elsewhere (maybe a keybind section that calls useMountItem via inventory scan). Let me grep more broadly for 'KeyR' and 'QuickMount'.
```

In vanilla, `jump` is the jump counter (ticks remaining while ascending). The local jumpHold decrements from jumpHeight. Key SetMount findings vs implementation `setMount`:

2. Reset only on Dismount. Implementation calls `this.reset()` when active then re-activates — resets fatigue/frame. Minor deviation: vanilla keeps fatigue when switching? Actually fatigue is a mount instance field, shared across mounts (one Mount per player). So switching mounts keeps fatigue value. Minor.

3. SetMount adds buff: `AddBuff(_data.buff, 3600)` — implementation deliberately doesn't (documented). Acceptable given single-source-of-truth approach, BUT the buff has side effects consumed elsewhere in vanilla (e.g., buff 90 etc. are mount buffs with TimeLeftDoesNotDecrease). The implementation consumes swimFree via buff id set. OK — documented as approximation.

4. SetMount type 44: velocity *= 0.2, dash reset — missing in implementation. Minor (PirateShip).

5. SetMount abilityCooldown floors for type 9 (20) and 46 (40) — missing. Minor.

7. And in Dismount, the player's size is restored to 20×42 (because heightBoost enlarged it while riding?). Does the implementation grow the player hitbox by heightBoost when riding? 122	export interface HappinessInput {
123	  npcVanillaId: number;
124	  /** NPC 无家（homeless） */
125	  homeless: boolean;
126	  /** NPC 当前中心 tile 距家 tile 距离 */
127	  distFromHome: number;
128	  /** 玩家（购物者）所在危险群系 */
129	  playerInCorrupt: boolean; playerInCrimson: boolean; playerInDungeon: boolean;
130	  /** 玩家群系 zone 快照（偏好判定用） */
131	  zones: BiomeZones;
132	  /** 同屋人数（<25 tile 内其它城镇 NPC，含不可同住门） */
133	  npcsInHouse: number;
134	  /** 村人数（25-120 tile） */
135	  npcsInVillage: number;
136	  /** 同屋目标 vanillaId 列表（<25，GetNearbyResidentNPCs 已过滤 37/368/453；
137	   *  重复 id 折叠成位图语义——原版 nearbyNPCsByType 布尔） */
138	  nearby: number[];
139	  /** 本 NPC 是否公主（663） */
140	  isPrincess: boolean;
141	  /** 公主动态抽 Love 的随机源（:22-36 Main.rand.Next） */
142	  rand?: () => number;
143	}
144	
145	export interface HappinessResult {
146	  /** 价格乘子（LimitAndRoundMultiplier 后 ∈ [0.75, 1.5]；1.0 = 无修正） */
147	  priceMul: number;
148	  /** 心情报告行（原版 HappinessReport 逐条；空 = 无按钮，ReportHappiness.Condition） */
149	  report: HappinessReportLine[];
150	}
151	
152	/** 心情文案分节名（AddHappinessReportText :196） */
153	export function npcMoodCategory(npcVanillaId: number): string {
154	  return `TownNPCMood_${NPC_NAME_BY_ID[npcVanillaId] ?? ''}`;
155	}
156	
157	/** GetShoppingSettings / ProcessMood 1:1 */
158	export function computeHappiness(inp: HappinessInput): HappinessResult {
159	  let mul = 1.0;
160	  const report: HappinessReportLine[] = [];
161	  const line = (sub: string, extra?: Partial<HappinessReportLine>) => {
162	    report.push({ key: `${npcMoodCategory(inp.npcVanillaId)}.${sub}`, ...extra });
163	  };
164	  // remix/旅行商(368)/骷髅商(453)/城镇宠物/IsNotReallyTownNPC(老人37)——1.0 且无报告（:107-110）
165	  if (inp.npcVanillaId === 368 || inp.npcVanillaId === 453 || inp.npcVanillaId === 37) {
166	    return { priceMul: 1, report: [] };
167	  }
168	  // 无家 / 离家 >120 / 危险群系 → 置 1000（:111-122；末尾钳回 1.5）
169	  if (inp.homeless) line('NoHome');                                  // RuinMoodIfHomeless :334-341
170	  else if (inp.distFromHome > 120) line('FarFromHome');              // IsFarFromHome :343-353
171	  for (const [flag, nameKey] of [
172	    [inp.playerInCorrupt, 'Corruption'], [inp.playerInCrimson, 'Crimson'], [inp.playerInDungeon, 'Dungeon'],
173	  ] as Array<[boolean, string]>) {
174	    if (flag) { line('HateBiome', { biomeNameKey: nameKey }); break; }  // IsPlayerInEvilBiomes :355-370（首命中）
175	  }
176	  if (inp.homeless || inp.distFromHome > 120
177	    || inp.playerInCorrupt || inp.playerInCrimson || inp.playerInDungeon) {
178	    mul = 1000;
179	  }
180	  // 拥挤/宽敞（:123-157）。公主：拥挤系数 1.0（报告照发）且不吃 LoveSpace
181	  const princess = inp.isPrincess;
182	  const crowdPer = princess ? 1.0 : 1.05;
183	  if (princess && inp.npcsInHouse < 2 && inp.npcsInVillage < 2) {
184	    line('HateLonely');                                              // :132-137
185	    mul = 1000;
186	  }
187	  if (inp.npcsInHouse > 3) {
188	    for (let i = 3; i < inp.npcsInHouse; i++) mul *= crowdPer;       // :138-143
189	    line(inp.npcsInHouse > 6 ? 'HateCrowded' : 'DislikeCrowded');
190	  }
191	  if (!princess && inp.npcsInHouse <= 2 && inp.npcsInVillage < 4) {
192	    line('LoveSpace');                                               // :153-157
193	    mul *= 0.95;
194	  }
195	  // 生物群系偏好（BiomePreferenceListTrait.ModifyShopPrice :40-55：命中中取好感最强一条）
196	  const prefs = BIOME_PREFS[inp.npcVanillaId];
197	  if (prefs) {
198	    let best: { level: 'Love' | 'Like' | 'Dislike' | 'Hate'; biome: BiomeKey } | null = null;
199	    for (const p of prefs) {
200	      if (inp.zones[p.biome] && (!best || AFFECTION[p.level] > AFFECTION[best.level])) best = p;
201	    }
202	    if (best) {
203	      line(`${best.level}Biome`, { biomeNameKey: BIOME_NAME_KEY[best.biome] });
204	      mul *= REL_MUL[best.level];
205	    }
206	  }
207	  // NPC 互相关系（AllPersonalitiesModifier.ModifyShopPrice_Relationships :12-506——全部命中连乘）
208	  const near = new Set(inp.nearby);
209	  if (princess) {
210	    // 公主无静态表：同屋池随机抽 3 个各 LoveNPCByTypeName ×0.88（:15-26；不足 3 全 Love）
211	    const pool = [...near];
212	    const rand = inp.rand ?? Math.random;
213	    for (let j = 0; j < 3 && pool.length > 0; j++) {
214	      const idx = Math.floor(rand() * pool.length) % pool.length;
215	      const target = pool.splice(idx, 1)[0];
216	      mul *= LOVE_MUL;
217	      line(`LoveNPC_${NPC_NAME_BY_ID[target] ?? ''}`, { npcId: target });
218	    }
219	  } else {
220	    if (near.has(663)) {                                             // :37-40
221	      mul *= LIKE_MUL;
222	      line('LikeNPC_Princess', { npcId: 663 });
223	    }
224	    for (const rel of NPC_RELATIONSHIPS[inp.npcVanillaId] ?? []) {
225	      if (near.has(rel.target)) {
226	        mul *= REL_MUL[rel.level];
227	        line(REL_KEY[rel.level], { npcId: rel.target });
228	      }
229	    }
230	  }
231	  // 无报告 → Content；LimitAndRoundMultiplier（:175-186）
232	  if (report.length === 0) line('Content');
233	  mul = Math.max(0.75, Math.min(1.5, mul));
234	  mul = Math.round(mul * 100) / 100;
235	  return { priceMul: mul, report };
236	}
237

295		private List<NPC> GetNearbyResidentNPCs(NPC npc, out int npcsWithinHouse, out int npcsWithinVillage)
296		{
297			List<NPC> list = new List<NPC>();
298			npcsWithinHouse = 0;
299			npcsWithinVillage = 0;
300			Vector2 value = new Vector2(npc.homeTileX, npc.homeTileY);
301			if (npc.homeless)
302			{
303				value = new Vector2(npc.Center.X / 16f, npc.Center.Y / 16f);
304			}
305			for (int i = 0; i < Main.maxNPCs; i++)
306			{
307				if (i == npc.whoAmI)
308				{
309					continue;
310				}
311				NPC nPC = Main.npc[i];
312				if (nPC.active && nPC.townNPC && !IsNotReallyTownNPC(nPC) && !WorldGen.TownManager.CanNPCsLiveWithEachOther_ShopHelper(npc, nPC))
313				{
314					Vector2 value2 = new Vector2(nPC.homeTileX, nPC.homeTileY);
315					if (nPC.homeless)
316					{
317						value2 = nPC.Center / 16f;
318					}
319					float num = Vector2.Distance(value, value2);
320					if (num < 25f)
321					{
322						list.Add(nPC);
323						npcsWithinHouse++;
324					}
325					else if (num < 120f)
326					{
327						npcsWithinVillage++;
328					}
329				}
330			}
331			return list;
332		}

347			if (Vector2.Distance(value, value2) > 120f)
348			{

350				return true;
351			}
352			return false;
353		}

368			}

16				Affection = affection;
17				Biome = biome;
18			}
19		}

21		private List<BiomePreference> _preferences;
22	
23		public BiomePreferenceListTrait()
24		{
25			_preferences = new List<BiomePreference>();
26		}

30			_preferences.Add(preference);
31		}

36		}

38		public void ModifyShopPrice(HelperInfo info, ShopHelper shopHelperInstance)
39		{
40			BiomePreference biomePreference = null;
41			for (int i = 0; i < _preferences.Count; i++)
42			{
43				BiomePreference biomePreference2 = _preferences[i];
44				if (biomePreference2.Biome.IsInBiome(info.player) && (biomePreference == null || biomePreference.Affection < biomePreference2.Affection))
45				{
46					biomePreference = biomePreference2;
47				}
48			}
49			if (biomePreference != null)
50			{
51				ApplyPreference(biomePreference, info, shopHelperInstance);
52			}
53		}
54	
55		private void ApplyPreference(BiomePreference preference, HelperInfo info, ShopHelper shopHelperInstance)
56		{
57			string nameKey = preference.Biome.NameKey;
58			switch (preference.Affection)
59			{
60			case AffectionLevel.Love:
61				shopHelperInstance.LoveBiome(nameKey);
62				break;
63			case AffectionLevel.Like:
64				shopHelperInstance.LikeBiome(nameKey);
65				break;
66			case AffectionLevel.Dislike:
67				shopHelperInstance.DislikeBiome(nameKey);
68				break;
69			case AffectionLevel.Hate:
70				shopHelperInstance.HateBiome(nameKey);
71				break;
72			}
73		}
74	
75		public IEnumerator<BiomePreference> GetEnumerator()
76		{
77			return _preferences.GetEnumerator();
78		}

81		{
82			return _preferences.GetEnumerator();
83		}
84	}

- ShoppingZone_BelowSurface = position.Y > worldSurface*16 (i.e., below the surface line — dirt layer or deeper).

- underground: `sc?.zoneDirtLayerHeight || sc?.zoneRockLayerHeight` — hmm. - forest: local = !corrupt && !crimson && !hallow && !snow && !jungle && !desert && !glowshroom && !beach && !dungeon && !rockLayerHeight — note: it also excludes rockLayerHeight (deeper) but doesn't exclude dirtLayerHeight; and it doesn't check BelowSurface at all for "forest" other than via rockLayerHeight. Vanilla excludes BelowSurface (i.e., both dirt and rock layers). So local "forest" would be true while standing in the dirt layer underground if no other zone — vanilla says false. Need to check local scene flag semantics.

  const zoneMarble = centerWall === 183 || centerWall === 178;
  const zoneHive = centerWall === 108 || centerWall === 86;
  const zoneGemCave = centerWall >= 48 && centerWall <= 53;
  const behindBackwall = centerWall > 0;

  // oceanDepths（WorldGen.cs:10978-10988：y > oceanLevel 恒假；否则 x 距边 < beachDistance(380)）
  const oceanTop = (worldSurface + rockLevel) / 2 + 40;
  const zoneBeach = tileY <= oceanTop && (tileX < BEACH_DISTANCE || tileX > w - BEACH_DISTANCE);

  return {
    tileX, tileY,
    belowSurface,
    // 0.3499999940395355 = C# 0.35f 的 float32 表示(:672-673 原文)——JS 双精度
    // 0.35 会在 worldSurface*0.35 恰为整数时把该行判错档(Math.fround 复原)
    zoneSkyHeight: tileY <= worldSurface * Math.fround(0.35),
    zoneOverworldHeight: tileY <= worldSurface && tileY > worldSurface * Math.fround(0.35),
    zoneDirtLayerHeight: tileY <= rockLevel && tileY > worldSurface,
    zoneRockLayerHeight: tileY <= underworldLayer && tileY > rockLevel,
    zoneUnderworldHeight,
    zoneCorrupt: evil >= CORRUPTION_THRESHOLD,
    zoneCrimson: blood >= CRIMSON_THRESHOLD,
    zoneHallow: holy >= HALLOW_THRESHOLD,
    zoneJungle: jungle >= JUNGLE_THRESHOLD && !zoneUnderworldHeight,
    zoneSnow: snow >= SNOW_THRESHOLD,
    zoneDesert,
    snowTileCount: snow,
    desertSandTileCount: desert, // DesertSandTileCount = max(0, SandTileCount - OceanSandTileCount)（L665，
    // 海洋沙 = isDesertBiomeSand ∩ oceanDepths 逐格计，扫描窗内与原版一致）
    graveyardTileCount: graveyard,
    hasSunflower,
    zoneGlowshroom: mushroom >= MUSHROOM_THRESHOLD,

Combined: up is held and has been released-re-pressed at least once? No: releaseUp is set true when up is released; controlUp && releaseUp means: up currently held, and it was released before (since last press). Actually releaseUp stays true until next press? In Terraria, releaseUp = true when the up key is NOT held... Let me not go down this hole; the common interpretation: hovering starts when you hold Up after having released it. The local proxy differs but is a documented approximation ("松键边沿"). It's approximately right for jump-based entry (vanilla hover entry is via holding Up while grounded → the tryStartHover nudges you off the ground). Minor.
